#### #2 `CalendarSpreadCheck` clamps remapped strikes and fabricates calendar breaches (Blocker)

- **Defect (verified in source):** `CalendarSpreadCheck::check` in `arbitrage/checks.rs` probes only the earlier row's strikes, maps each to `f2 * exp(k)` and reads the later row with `get_surface_vol_clamped`, so an out-of-grid strike is compared at a different moneyness. It also repeats the last forward when `forwards` is short (as does `ButterflyCheck`) and skips nonpositive forwards silently.
- **Record remedy:**
  1. Delete `CalendarSpreadCheck` and the repeat-last forward fallback; the calendar family runs only inside `check_surface` with per-expiry forwards. MODELS-ANALYTIC-20 d1, d2
  2. Compare adjacent expiries at fixed `k = log(K/F)` in total variance `w = T sigma^2`. MODELS-ANALYTIC-20 d11
  3. For each adjacent pair, intersect the two closed normalized-strike supports `[K_min/F, K_max/F]`. Probe the ascending exact union of both rows' normalized knots inside the intersection, endpoints included. Compare original positive ratios without early rounding; deduplicate exact equality only. Wings outside the overlap are out of the declared scope and each pair's overlap is disclosed in coverage. MODELS-ANALYTIC-20 d12
  4. At each probe evaluate each row at its own matched cash strike with the surface's declared strike interpolation, using the complete ratio product `K_j = K * F_j / F_i` (exact K when forwards are equal) and classifying support before rounding. Use strict lookup: an exact knot returns its stored quote, an interior point its adjacent pair, nothing is clamped. Keep the normalized probe identity and both evaluated strikes in the finding. Unresolved geometry is an error. MODELS-ANALYTIC-20 d13; MODELS-ANALYTIC-19 d5; MODELS-ANALYTIC-16 d6, d7
  5. A probe that originates from a row's own knot reads that row's stored node by index, not by recomputing `(K/F)*F`, so an endpoint cannot round outside the grid and fail strict lookup. The existing `get_surface_vol` already returns the stored sigma at an exact knot in both interpolation modes (checked in `source.rs`), so no change to it is needed. MODELS-ANALYTIC-20 d13; MODELS-ANALYTIC-16 d7
  6. Breach when the signed `w_later - w_earlier` is strictly below `-total_variance_abs_tol`. MODELS-ANALYTIC-20 d14
  7. Coverage: a positive-width overlap with all union probes evaluated is complete; a singleton overlap is tested and reported as incomplete; an empty overlap or a single-expiry surface is incomplete (not a silent pass). MODELS-ANALYTIC-20 d14, d21
- **Differs from the old plan:** The old plan skipped out-of-range strikes but still probed only the earlier row's knots; the record requires the union of both rows' knots on the intersection (its third fixture shows earlier-row knots alone miss a later-row breach) and makes missing overlap an `Incomplete` status.
- **Files:** as #1 (same module, bindings, schemas and UI). The strict lookup is the existing `get_surface_vol` in `finstack-quant/models/src/volatility/source.rs`; `get_surface_vol_clamped` loses its arbitrage callers.
- **Tests:**
  - Add the record's fixture: K=(80,100,120), F=(100,110), early w=(0.04,0.04,0.04), later w=(0.06,0.03,0.06), TotalVariance mode gives common knots (4/5, 10/11, 1, 12/11) and differences (0.008, -0.01, 0.005, 0.02); one breach, found only through the later row's knot.
  - Add: point-only overlap (tested, `Incomplete`); disjoint supports (`Incomplete`, no finding); a surface whose only old "breach" came from clamping reports none; equal forwards reuse exact strikes. MODELS-ANALYTIC-20 d27
  - Re-derive: `calendar_spread_violation_detected` and `clean_smile_passes_butterfly_and_calendar` in `arbitrage/mod.rs`; the calendar rows of the FNV digest test.
- **Deferred:** none beyond #1's list.

#### #9 Dupire time derivative uses an equal-step secant on non-uniform expiry grids (Major)

- **Defect (verified in source):** `finite_diff_time` in `finstack-quant/models/src/volatility/dupire.rs` returns `(w_{i+1} - w_{i-1}) / (T_{i+1} - T_{i-1})` at interior expiries, ignoring the centre sample. The first and last expiry already use the adjacent two-point secant, which is what the record wants. The audit calls the extractor `LocalVolSurface::build`; the real function is `LocalVolSurface::from_implied_vol`. Also present in the same kernel: the remap is `forwards[idx] * k.exp()`, strike derivatives are taken in cash strike then chain-ruled, edge strikes use a one-sided first difference, and `1e-14` deadbands and a near-uniform switch are applied.
- **Record remedy:**
  1. Interior expiry: with `h_- = T_i - T_(i-1)`, `h_+ = T_(i+1) - T_i` and adjacent slopes `d_-`, `d_+`, use `(h_+ d_- + h_- d_+) / (h_- + h_+)` at fixed k. MODELS-ANALYTIC-19 d9
  2. First and last expiry: keep the adjacent two-point secant; a two-row source uses it at both ends. Do not add a 3-point one-sided stencil. MODELS-ANALYTIC-19 d10
  3. Matched strikes in neighbouring rows use the complete ratio product `K * F_j / F_i` from original coordinates, exact K for equal forwards, with support classified before rounding; an out-of-range flat sample passes only the finite effective endpoint to the lookup. Each neighbour row is sampled at its exact expiry with the stored strike interpolation, and w is built from that row's own time and quote. The kernel keeps its existing two support modes (strict for the scanner, flat for the extractor). MODELS-ANALYTIC-19 d5, d6
  4. Strike derivatives: take the nearest three source nodes (prior, centre, next inside; first three at the left edge; last three at the right) in log-moneyness built from log ratios of original strikes. With secants u, v and `A = (v-u)/(k_2-k_0)`: `w_k = u + A((k-k_0)+(k-k_1))`, `w_kk = 2A`. Same formulas at edges and inside; delete the cash-strike derivative and chain rule in `finite_diff_strike`. MODELS-ANALYTIC-19 d11, d12
  5. Remove the `1e-14` spacing deadbands and the near-uniform branch; reject unresolved geometry and failed arithmetic; exact constants give zero derivatives; keep scale so a representable local sigma survives an intermediate underflow. MODELS-ANALYTIC-19 d13
  6. Density factor `g = (1 - k w_k/(2w))^2 - (w_k^2/4)(1/w + 1/4) + w_kk/2`, defined only for centre `w > 0`; neighbours may be zero. Replace the `MIN_TOTAL_VARIANCE` floor with the `w > 0` test. MODELS-ANALYTIC-19 d14, d15
  7. One kernel returns signed `w_T`, `g` and a three-way outcome (computed, matched neighbour unavailable under strict support, failed computation), shared by the scanner and the extractor. MODELS-ANALYTIC-19 d16
  8. Scanner (replaces `LocalVolDensityCheck`): over an explicit rectangular index range, with strict support and the unsmoothed source, report computed `g <= 0` as a failure of the positive-density requirement and computed `w_T < 0` as a time-slope failure, independently. Zero slope is allowed. Zero centre or too few strike nodes makes the density probe unavailable; a missing time stencil or out-of-support matched strike makes the slope unavailable. No tolerance and no `w_T/g` ratio test. MODELS-ANALYTIC-20 d15, d16, d17, d18
  9. Extractor `LocalVolSurface::from_implied_vol`: require centre `w > 0`, `g > 0` and `w_T >= 0`; exact zero slope gives sigma zero; otherwise the positive root of `w_T/g`. Reject any negative computed slope. Remove the `DEFAULT_ARBITRAGE_TOLERANCE` slack and `dw_dt.max(0.0)`. This is forced here because step #1.4 deletes that constant. MODELS-ANALYTIC-19 d15
- **Differs from the old plan:** The old plan added 3-point one-sided boundary stencils; the record keeps the two-point secant there (its T=(1,2,3) fixture gives +0.001 by secant and -0.043 by one-sided quadratic). The record also moves strike derivatives to log-moneyness and removes the extraction tolerance, neither of which the old plan covered.
- **Files:**
  - Rust: `finstack-quant/models/src/volatility/dupire.rs`, `volatility/local_vol.rs` (`from_implied_vol`; `from_implied_vol_smoothed` inherits through it), `volatility/arbitrage/checks.rs`.
  - Python/WASM: no signature change for local vol; update the documented error conditions in `finstack-quant-py/finstack_quant/models/volatility/__init__.pyi` (`LocalVolSurface.from_implied_vol*`) and the JSDoc on `localVolFromImpliedVol*` in `finstack-quant-wasm/src/api/models/volatility.rs`.
  - Parity/schema: none for local vol. The `LocalVolSurface.grid_shape = "literal"` exclusion in `finstack-quant-py/parity_contract.toml` stays as is. Rust `grid_shape` returns the lengths of the `expiries` and `strikes` wire fields and does nothing else; the ledger's rule admits `literal` for "an accessor that reads a count ... straight off wire fields" on a `types`-map class, which `LocalVolSurface` is, the same as the accepted `FactorTimeSeries.num_dates` and `YieldPcaView.num_components` entries. No WASM `localVolGridShape` export is added. This slice renames no axis; see the d18-21 line under Deferred.
  - Callers: none in `finstack-quant/valuations/**`, `calibration`, `scenarios` or `portfolio`. In-crate consumer: `finstack-quant/models/src/monte_carlo/process/local_vol.rs` (test builds a surface with `from_implied_vol`).
- **Tests:**
  - Add the record's fixtures: T=(0.25,0.5,1.25), w=0.04T+0.02T^2 gives interior 0.06 (old code 0.07) and first-row secant 0.055; T=(1,2,3), w=(0.01,0.011,0.1) gives first-row 0.001; k=(-0.2,-0.05,0.25), w=0.04+0.03k+0.02k^2 recovers `w_k=0.03+0.04k`, `w_kk=0.04` at all three nodes; flat sigma 1e-170 is recovered. (Fixtures from record 19's arithmetic table; the kernel part of MODELS-ANALYTIC-19 d25.)

#### Open questions for the owner

1. **Call-price family (MODELS-ANALYTIC-20 d5-10): defer or include?** Deferring leaves no sampled price check between this slice and the one that lands records 02 and 15; the Dupire density probe is the only strike-direction check meanwhile. Including it makes this slice depend on those two records' checked pricing and price-grid arithmetic.
2. **Forward binding shape (MODELS-ANALYTIC-20 d2).** The record requires a forward bound to each exact expiry "and its underlying and strike units" plus an optional source clock, but does not give a wire shape. Proposal: a list of `{expiry, forward}` pairs matched by exact equality to the surface expiries; units and clock deferred to the record 16 source type. Confirm.
3. **Extraction now rejects tiny negative slopes (MODELS-ANALYTIC-19 d15).** Surfaces that extract today with a slope in (-1e-10, 0) will error. This is forced by deleting the shared tolerance; confirm it lands here and not with the deferred extraction request.
4. **Extractor wing policy.** Until MODELS-ANALYTIC-19 d4 lands, `from_implied_vol` keeps flat implied wings implicitly while the scanner is strict. Acceptable as an interim, or should d3-4 and d7 (explicit region, wing choice and the single extraction with optional smoothing, which re-signature `from_implied_vol` and retire `from_implied_vol_smoothed` in Rust, Python and WASM) be pulled in? Read literally, clause 2 of the scope rule pulls them in because step #9.9 edits `from_implied_vol`; they are deferred here because nothing this slice builds is removed when they land.
5. **Report summaries on the wire (MODELS-ANALYTIC-20 d24).** The record allows status and counts to be derived on restore or serialized and verified. Step #1.10 serializes and verifies them so WASM and the UI read them from the plain object. The alternative is a derived Rust method, which needs a WASM free-function twin and a `renames` ledger entry per summary. Confirm.

#### #8 `convert_atm_volatility` returns the source vol unchanged at T=0 (Major)

- **Defect (verified in source):** `finstack-quant/models/src/volatility/convert.rs` — `convert_atm_volatility` has `if time_to_expiry == 0.0 { return Ok(vol); }` before any forward-domain check, so a Normal quote is returned as a Lognormal one. Also present on the same path: zero source vol is rejected (`vol <= 0.0`), a near-equal-shift identity (`(s1 - s2).abs() < 1e-12`), a default `BrentSolver::new(1e-12, 100)` with `compute_initial_guess`, a `1e-16` vol floor and no premium replay. **`convert_strike_volatility` / `convert_volatility` do not exist anywhere in the repo** (git grep empty); that half of the finding and of the old plan step is dropped, and record 03 has no code to govern in this slice.
- **Record remedy:**
  1.   4. Shortcuts: literal zero source sigma returns canonical zero; identical law returns sigma unchanged, where two Black-family laws are identical only when effective A agrees exactly (unshifted and shift=0 are the same law). Delete the `< 1e-12` shift shortcut. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-22 d5`
  5. Source premium from the analytic ATM laws with h=√T: Normal `P = σ_N h/√(2π)`; Black `P = A erf(u)`, `u = σ_B h/(2√2)`; small-u form `P = A σ_B h/√(2π) × R(u)` with factor one for u ≤ 2⁻²⁷.   8. Invert on the smaller probability: `u_t = erf_inv(P/A_t)` if P ≤ D else `erfc_inv(D/A_t)`; `σ_t = 2√2 u_t / h`; for P/A_t ≤ 2⁻²⁷ use `√(2π) P/(A_t h)`. #### #16 (SVI part) `calibrate_svi` admits butterfly arbitrage (Major)

- **Defect (verified in source):** `finstack-quant/models/src/volatility/svi.rs` — `calibrate_svi_with_solver` never evaluates Durrleman g on the fitted slice, uses a fixed heuristic seed (b=0.1, ρ=0, m=0, σ=0.1), and gates on a variance-derived approximate vol RMSE (`MAX_VOL_RMSE = 0.005`). `calibrate_svi` hardcodes `LevenbergMarquardtSolver::new(1e-12, 1e-12, 300)`, and the fit re-wraps every solver error as `Error::Validation`, losing its category. Partly not as the audit states: a Lee check **does** exist in `SviParams::validate` (`b(1+|ρ|) ≤ 2 + 1e-12`), and it is called on the fit result. Already in place: checked `SviParams::new` and strict restoration through `TryFrom<RawSviParams>` (`deny_unknown_fields`), and a fallible `implied_vol`; the five fields are still `pub`, and `total_variance` and `durrleman_g` return bare `f64`. The only Durrleman scan today is the conditional 201-point scan in `finstack-quant/calibration/src/targets/svi.rs` over the target-strike range ±1, which only runs when `validation.check_arbitrage` is set. A violation never blocks publication: `solve` still returns the surface, and `attach_validation_result` marks the report failed under `ValidationMode::Error` or only writes a `validation_warning` under `Warn`.
- **Record remedy:**
  1. Parameter state: make `SviParams` fields private with `get_*` accessors; keep `SviParams::new` as the one checked constructor and keep restoration routed through it (both exist), five named wire fields unchanged, unknown/duplicate/missing rejected; delete public struct-literal construction and the public `validate` as a post-hoc call. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d2`
  2. Admission rules in that constructor: exact minimum `w_min = a + b s √((1−ρ)(1+ρ)) ≥ 0` with no `-1e-14` slack (b=0 needs a ≥ 0); **asymmetric wings: left `b(1−ρ) ≤ 2`, right `b(1+ρ) < 2`**, compared on represented inputs with no `1e-12` slack. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d3-4`
  3. Evaluation the fit relies on becomes fallible: checked log-moneyness from K and F without pre-dividing; `total_variance` in the stable form `w = w_min + b (x+ρR)² / (L + s q)`; `implied_vol` (already fallible) as `sqrt(w/T)` in scale with T>0, no floor. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d5-7`
  4. `durrleman_g` returns a checked result using analytic `w' = b(ρ + x/R)`, `w'' = b s²/R³`; w=0 is an error, not `NEG_INFINITY`. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d8-9`
  5. Replace `butterfly_violation(k_lo, k_hi, n, tol)` with an explicit scan over caller-supplied points (≥ 2, finite, strictly increasing) and a finite tolerance ≥ 0, returning minimum g, its first coordinate and the sample count; invalid grid is an error (today it returns `None`, i.e. success). Rust-only. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d10-11`
  6. One fit entry point (keep the short name `calibrate_svi`; delete `calibrate_svi_with_solver`) taking a request with: ≥ 5 paired finite positive strikes and vols, F, T, one explicit raw seed (positive minimum variance, both slopes strictly in (0,2)), LM residual and step tolerances, attempt limit, absolute annual-vol error cap, density scan points and tolerance. Sort by strike keeping original order for errors; reject duplicate strikes and strikes collapsing to one represented k; validate the scan and require its endpoints to cover the quotes before solving. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d12, d14`
  7. Objective unchanged in kind: unweighted residuals `w(k_i) − T σ_i²`. **No butterfly penalty in the solve.** `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d13`
  8. Coordinates: `w_min = exp(u)`, `s = exp(v)`, `L = 2 logistic(l)`, `H = 2 logistic(h)`, m free; `b = (L+H)/2`, `ρ = (H−L)/(H+L)`, `a = w_min − b s √((1−ρ)(1+ρ))`; verify the represented raw tuple stays strictly interior. Replaces the `exp`/`tanh` map on (a, b, ρ, m, σ). `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d15`
  9. One caller-seeded LM solve per CORE-NUMERICS-06 with validated callback candidates and the real attempt budget; hard failures propagate. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d16`. The solver this needs is already in `finstack-quant/core/src/math/solver_multi.rs` (landed in `53b0385d69`; no core change in this slice): validated `LevenbergMarquardtSolver::new(residual_tolerance, step_tolerance, max_iterations) -> Result` (`CORE-NUMERICS-06 d2`); fallible `FnMut(&[f64], &mut [f64]) -> Result<()>` residuals with poisoned buffers and first-error propagation (`d3`); three-point central differences on the actual spacings (`d4`); augmented column-pivoted Householder QR (`d5`); `max_iterations` bounds damped attempts and `LmStats.iterations` counts them (`d10`). Use `solve_least_squares`; return the callback's own error from the residual closure and delete the `map_err` that turns solver errors into `Error::Validation`.
  10. Equal-vol shortcut: if all input sigmas are equal, return a = T σ², b=ρ=m=0, s=1 with zero LM attempts and the same acceptance. This resolves the audit's "degenerates on flat smiles". `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d17`
  11. Judge the final represented candidate: rebuild through the constructor, recompute the objective; a finite candidate from step, damping or attempt termination (`LmTerminationReason::{StepTooSmall, DampingLimit, MaxIterations}`, all returned as `Ok` today) stays eligible. **Termination reason alone does not reject.** `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d18`; `CORE-NUMERICS-06 d9, d13`
  12. Acceptance gate A: replay annual Black sigma at every original quote; every absolute error ≤ the supplied cap; report signed errors in input order, max abs and annual-vol RMSE. Delete `MAX_VOL_RMSE` and the `avg_w` proxy. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d19`
  13. Acceptance gate B: scan the exact union of supplied points and quote coordinates; **any g < −tolerance rejects the fit** even when quotes fit. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d20`
  14. Return a fit result (parameters, F, T, controls, seed, literal solve/analytic status, work, objective norm, quote errors, scan result) instead of `(SviParams, usize)`; status and work come from `LmTerminationReason` and `LmStats` (attempts, accepted steps, residual and Jacobian evaluations). `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d21`
  15. Calibration caller: `calibration/src/targets/svi.rs` passes explicit seed, tolerances, cap and scan from its step params, drops `global_config.create_lm_solver()` for this target, deletes its own 201-point scan with ±1 margins and `-1e-10`, and stores the fit evidence in `FittedSlice`. The model fit now rejects a breaching slice, so the step fails instead of publishing with a report flag. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d22`; `CALIBRATION-TARGETS-012 d2-4, d6`. Records silent on whether the seed and controls are per step or per expiry; simplest consistent choice: one required seed and one control set on `SviSurfaceParams`, applied to every expiry.
  16. Hosts: Python `SviParams` (checked constructor; `total_variance` and `durrleman_g` now raising, `implied_vol` already does), Python `calibrate_svi` and WASM `calibrateSvi` taking one params object and returning the fit result; WASM `sviTotalVariance` and `sviDurrlemanG` propagate evaluation errors (`sviImpliedVol` already does). `MODELS-ANALYTIC-18 d24`
- **Differs from the old plan:** The plan penalised butterfly violations inside the LM objective and rejected on LM termination; the record keeps the unweighted objective, says termination alone decides nothing, and rejects only on the post-fit sampled density gate and the native-vol replay. The plan's symmetric `b(1+|ρ|) ≤ 2` is replaced by left ≤ 2 / right < 2 (already partly in `validate`). The record also forces explicit seed and controls, deleting both current entry-point signatures.

Step 4 also needs the unit forward Black law of Slice 3 #14 (which removes `df` from `closed_form::forward::black76_price`).
- **Record remedy:** the K₀ patch is moot because the record removes the K₀ split.
  1. New signature: strikes, F, T, one fallible vol-by-strike callback bound to source and expiry by the adapter, an explicit wing mode, a mesh tolerance, wing budgets and work limits; returns a typed result. **Delete `bs_price_fn` and `risk_free_rate`.** `MODELS-ANALYTIC-21 d1-3`. Record silent on the function name; keep `carr_madan_forward_variance` unless the owner asks for a rename.
  2. Validate before any callback: ≥ 3 finite positive strictly increasing strikes with one strictly below and one strictly above F; finite positive F and T; wing mode, tolerances, budgets and work limits. No minimum strike or moneyness threshold. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-21 d4, d8`
  3. Split at the exact forward: keep F if it is a knot, else insert the represented F; sample it once; **one shared ATM premium** (put = call at K=F); the anchor correction is exactly zero, so delete `demeterfi_anchor`. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-21 d5`
  4. Premiums: put below F, call above F, undiscounted unit Black premium on (F, T, sampled σ) from the checked record-02 forward law; no discount factor in the strip. σ must be finite ≥ 0; **zero is admitted** and gives zero premium; first source or model error propagates with its strike. Delete `MIN_USABLE_VOL`. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-21 d6-7`
  5. Quadrature: per panel `h/2 × (Q_a/K_a² + Q_b/K_b²)` on the forward-split mesh; put and call panels meet at F; outer endpoints keep their own half-panel weight. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-21 d9`
  6. One midpoint refinement per panel; each strike evaluated once in ascending order and reused; return the fine estimate; require `|fine − coarse|` (annualised) ≤ the supplied mesh tolerance, else error; report the difference. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-21 d10-11`
  7. Keep panel contributions in scale and sum in deterministic compensated order; a lost positive or unresolved result errors. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-21 d12`
  8. Wing policy is required: `FiniteStrip` (both wings reported omitted, amount unknown) or full flat-Black wings to 0 and ∞ using each edge's own sampled σ with no further source queries. Delete `WING_EXTENSION_FACTOR`, `WING_SUBDIVISIONS`, `MIN_WING_LOG_MONEYNESS`, `wing_tail_integral` and the narrow-grid warning. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-21 d13`
  9. Full-wing amount per edge: with `ell = log(K_edge/F)`, `v = σ_edge √T`, `s = v √r`: `σ_edge² × ∫₀¹ Φ(η(ell/s + s/2)) dr`, η=+1 lower put, −1 upper call; adaptive Simpson; split the upper wing at `r = 2 ell/v²` when inside (0,1); separate absolute tolerances, max depth and evaluation limits per wing; at most three panels preflighted; a zero computed wing with positive edge σ is an error. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-21 d14-18`. Each panel is one call to the 0b-a `adaptive_simpson` with a fallible integrand, that wing's tolerance (the upper tolerance split as `tol/2` and `tol − tol/2` across its two panels), its `max_depth` and its `max_evals`; the wing callback count in the diagnostics is the integrator's actual count. #### #17b Rough Heston input validation (Major)

- **Defect (verified in source):** `finstack-quant/models/src/volatility/rough_heston.rs` — the audit's `RoughHestonParams::characteristic_function` and `rough_heston_call_price` do not exist; the real surface is `RoughHestonFourierParams::{char_func, price_european, implied_vol}` plus public `FractionalRiccatiSolver`. Inputs are checked but failures are sentinels: `price_european` returns `f64::NAN`, `char_func` returns NaN complex, `implied_vol` returns `None`. Real defect: `t <= 0.0` (including **negative t**) returns intrinsic before spot/strike positivity or r/q finiteness is checked. Fixed contour `[1e-8, 200]` and implicit `PRICE_SCALE_TOLERANCE = 1e-6` are as the audit says.
- **Record remedy:**
  1. Parameter state: six flat private fields of the type's own (v0, κ, θ, σ_v, ρ, H), validated in the constructor and in strict restoration under the rough Fourier domain (finite positive v0, κ, θ, σ_v; ρ in (−1,1); H in (0,½)). Today the constructor delegates to `HestonParams::new` and stores a flattened public `model: HestonParams`; remove both, so the type no longer embeds or calls `HestonParams`. The record states that "the broader classical parameter domain in record 08 does not determine rough Fourier admission", and today's `HestonParams::validate` happens to apply the same strict rules, so this step changes no admitted value and neither waits on nor is moved by the shared `HestonParams` domain change (Slice 3 #13b). Wire shape unchanged (`RawRoughHestonFourierParams` is already flat with `deny_unknown_fields`). `MODELS-ANALYTIC-12 d1, d3`
  2. Add validated `RoughHestonControls { price_abs_tol > 0 finite, price_rel_tol in (0,1) }`; delete `PRICE_SCALE_TOLERANCE`, `CHARACTERISTIC_TOLERANCE` and other implicit tolerances. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-12 d3`
  3. `price_european(market {S, K, r, q, T, call/put}, controls) -> Result<f64>`; retire public `FractionalRiccatiSolver` and `char_func`; Rust-only. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-12 d4`
  4. Validate before the payoff branch: finite positive S and K, finite T ≥ 0, finite r and q, valid state and controls. **Exact T=0 returns intrinsic `max(η(S−K),0)`; negative T errors.** `MODELS-ANALYTIC-12 d5`
  5. Delete `RoughHestonFourierParams::implied_vol` (no callers outside its own file by git grep; three in-file tests use it); a caller wanting IV uses the checked premium with record 03's inverse. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-12 d24`
  6. Equity caller `finstack-quant/valuations/equity/src/equity_option/rough_heston_fourier_pricer.rs`: read the two tolerances from `model_config.rough_heston_fourier` on the instrument override carrier (`ModelConfig` in `finstack-quant/valuations/core/src/instruments/pricing_overrides.rs`; the field does not exist today), keep them apart from the six market scalars, propagate the Rust error instead of feeding NaN into `Money::from_model_value`; regenerate schemas and generated types. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-12 d25`
  7. The controls in steps 2-3 are consumed only by the acceptance rule `E_U + E_time + E_mesh + R ≤ min(τ_abs, price_rel_tol·Q)` and its refinement loop, which the current fixed-grid step-doubling loop cannot express. Under the scope test the numerical engine comes with them: normalized half-contour Riccati and local root (d6-8, d11-12), product-integration weights (d9-10), exponent assembly and OTM time value (d13-15), tail enclosure and cutoff selection (d16-18), order-16 composite GL from zero (d19), two-axis stencil and acceptance (d20-21), work caps (d22), refinement and failure taxonomy (d23). `MODELS-ANALYTIC-12 d6-23` — see owner question 1.
- **Differs from the old plan:** The plan required `t > 0` and added `is_finite` guards to three function names that do not exist. The record keeps T=0 as a valid intrinsic return, rejects only negative T, turns NaN/None into `Result`, deletes two of the three public entry points and the IV helper, and requires explicit price tolerances.
- **Files:** Rust `finstack-quant/models/src/volatility/rough_heston.rs`, `finstack-quant/valuations/core/src/instruments/pricing_overrides.rs` (`ModelConfig`); test callers `models/src/monte_carlo/discretization/rough_heston.rs`, `models/tests/vol_models_quantlib_tests.rs`, `models/tests/model_schema_contract.rs` / Python: none / WASM: none directly (`finstack-quant-wasm/schemas/host/1/valuation_result.schema.json` and `types/valuation-result.d.ts` only carry the model key) / parity: ledger entry for the override carrier field if it is host-visible / schema `finstack-quant/models/schemas/models/1/rough_heston_fourier_params.schema.json` (unchanged shape), valuations override-carrier schema and UI generated types for the new `model_config.rough_heston_fourier` / callers: the equity pricer above (only production caller).
- **Tests:** Negative T errors; T=0 intrinsic after full validation (bad spot at T=0 now errors); NaN inputs error. If the engine is in scope: record references (H=0.25 L_z at u=2.5 ≈ −0.039898590639+0.002546774559i; zero vol-of-vol total variance 0.0775758933652; unit-ATM U=200 truncation bias 0.00159154611520). Re-derive `valuations/tests/instruments/equity_option/test_alt_models.rs` (`rough_heston_fourier_*`), the MC/Fourier comparison in `monte_carlo/discretization/rough_heston.rs` and `vol_models_quantlib_tests.rs`. `MODELS-ANALYTIC-12 d26`
- **Deferred:** d2 (clock and dimension statement, docs only). Everything else is either in steps 1-6 or in step 7 pending the owner's answer.

**Addresses:** #3, #4 (Blockers), #16 (SabrSmile and VolCube parts)  **Records:** MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 (`models-analytic/sabr-expansions.md`), MODELS-ANALYTIC-14 (`models-analytic/sabr-calibration.md`), MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 (`models-analytic/sabr-smile-repair.md`), MODELS-ANALYTIC-16 (`models-analytic/volatility-source-interpolation.md`), CORE-MARKET-16 (`core-market/sabr-cube-data.md`, where 13 d21 delegates)

**Verified:** 2026-11-18 against `40c9036fdc` (source, citations, counts). Not read decision by decision: MODELS-ANALYTIC-02, MODELS-ANALYTIC-03, the core distribution-functions record and calibration-targets 11 and 14, which this slice only points to; CORE-NUMERICS-06 and CORE-NUMERICS-05 were checked for the decisions 14 d10 and d17 rely on (06 d2, d3, d5, d9, d10; 05 d2, d3, d4), not in full.

**Split into four sub-slices, in this order. Each ends with the workspace building, all three hosts in parity and its own `slice-gate`.**

| Sub-slice | Content | Why it stands alone |
|---|---|---|
| 2a | Parameter type, one `implied_vol(.., convention)`, new formula, every scalar caller; required convention on `SabrSmile` construction and on the calibration request; `SabrShift` deleted (#3, #4) | After it no code infers a quote convention from β. The cube evaluators keep their signatures (they already take a convention), but `models/src/volatility/source.rs` calls five APIs 2a deletes, so its port to the one entry point and the cube arm of `VolSource::get_convention` land here (#3 step 10) |
| 2b | Calibration controls, objective, solver, pin and outcome (rest of record 14) | Needs 2a's quote, partials and request; nothing in 2c/2d depends on it |
| 2c | Smile axis, delta proxy, grid check and repair (#16 smile) | Needs 2a only |
| 2d | Cube off-grid evaluation and materializers (#16 cube) | Needs 2a only |

2a cannot be split further: the formula, the convention argument and the removal of the beta snap all change the same function, and a formula-only step would keep a beta-derived convention the record deletes (13 d22). The smile and the calibrator both call the scalar quote, so 2a also gives them their final required convention input (15 d1–2, 14 d1–3); otherwise they would need a private β-derived convention for one sub-slice, which is the stopgap the record removes. Their remaining decisions wait for 2b and 2c.

#### #3 `implied_vol_normal` accepts T ≤ 0 and reflects negative ATM forwards (Blocker)

- **Defect (verified in source):** Not reproducible as the audit states it. In `finstack-quant/models/src/volatility/sabr/expansion.rs`, `implied_vol_normal_unchecked` returns NaN for `t <= 0.0` and for `beta > 0` with a non-positive shifted forward or strike before it reaches the ATM shortcut, and `implied_vol_normal` turns NaN into a validation error. So `t = -0.5` and `F = K = -0.02` with β > 0 are both rejected today. What is real: the rejection is a generic "non-finite" message produced by a NaN sentinel; the outer guard exempts near-ATM inputs with a `1e-12` tolerance; `atm_vol_normal` still carries `f.abs().max(1e-10)` (dead for negative forwards, live as a floor); β below `1e-4` is silently treated as β = 0, which changes alpha's units; a zero vol passes (`v >= 0.0`).
- **Record remedy (sub-slice 2a):**
  1. Delete `implied_vol_normal`, `implied_vol_lognormal`, `implied_vol_normal_unchecked`, `atm_vol_normal`, `atm_lognormal` and `normal_beta_zero`. Keep one `SabrParameters::implied_vol(forward, strike, time_to_expiry, convention) -> Result<f64>` taking the existing `VolatilityConvention` (variants `Normal`, `Lognormal`, `ShiftedLognormal { shift }` keep their names and wire forms). MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d4, d22, d23
  2. Convention/shift compatibility, checked by exact numeric equality: Normal allows shift present or absent; `Lognormal` requires absent shift; `ShiftedLognormal { shift }` requires a stored shift equal to it, including explicit zero. A mismatch is a validation error. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d4
  3. Validate the whole request before any limit or shortcut: finite F, K; finite T > 0 (T = 0 rejected). Exact β = 0 with Normal output admits signed F and K and skips translated levels. Every other case requires F+s and K+s in (0, MAX], with s = 0 when absent, checked from the original inputs. A Normal output with β > 0 still needs the positive backbone domain. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d5; CORE-MARKET-16 d4
  4. Remove `BETA_SNAP_TOL` and every use (21 across three files). β = 0 and β = 1 are literal; a small positive β uses the supplied β. ATM is exact F = K; constant scale is exact ν = 0. No near-ATM tolerance branch. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d6; CORE-MARKET-16 d1, d4
  5. Failure semantics: parameter, coordinate and convention mismatches are validation errors; a lost-to-zero, out-of-range or unresolved result is a computation error. Remove the NaN sentinels, the `alpha.abs() < 1e-14 → 0` shortcut, the `1e-14`/`1e-10` floors and the zero-vol acceptance. Result must be strictly positive and finite. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d17
  6. Replace the models-local `SabrParameters` struct (five `pub` fields, its own `validate`) with a wrapper whose checked payload is core `SabrParameterData`: α > 0, β in [0,1], ν ≥ 0, ρ in the open interval (−1,1), optional finite signed shift with `Some(0)` distinct from `None`. One constructor with the literal parameters and optional shift, getters only, strict flat serde (required alpha/beta/nu/rho, optional shift, unknown and duplicate fields rejected). MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d2, d22; CORE-MARKET-16 d1
  7. Delete `new_with_shift`, `rates_standard`, `normal`, `lognormal`, `shifted_normal`, `shifted_lognormal`, `equity_default`, `rates_default`, `quote_convention`, `supports_negative_rates`. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d22
  8. `effective_rates` and `validate_inputs`: record silent on these names. Both contradict d5/d7 as public helpers (separately rounded shifted levels; domain check without a convention). Simplest consistent fix: delete them from the public surface and fold the check into step 3. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d5, d7 (by implication)
  9. Swaption consumer (`valuations/rates/src/swaption/types/swaption.rs`, `resolve_volatility_quote`): build the convention from `vol_model` and the stored shift as it does now, call the one `implied_vol` with it, and carry convention and shift through the existing `ResolvedVolatility`. Delete the `quote_convention() == Normal` rejection: β = 0 with a Black request in its positive domain is admitted. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d4, d6, d21
  10. Cube source (`models/src/volatility/source.rs`), forced here because it calls `implied_vol_normal`, `implied_vol_lognormal` and `quote_convention()`, builds a `SabrParameters` literal and reads its `shift` field. `cube_vol` and `get_cube_vol_clamped` call the one `implied_vol` with the caller's convention under 13 d4: Normal on any tuple; `Lognormal` only when the tuple has no shift (a Black request on a shifted tuple is a validation error, where today it returns a shifted quote); `ShiftedLognormal { shift }` accepted when it equals the tuple's shift exactly, so `normal_output`'s refusal of a shifted request goes now. The blended tuple goes through the checked constructor. `VolSource::get_convention` returns an error for a cube instead of reading β, and the cube arm of `get_vol` drops its `get_convention` guard and takes the convention from its caller. `resolve_volatility` in `valuations/core/src/instruments/common_impl/vol_resolution.rs` passes its `VolatilityRequest.convention` to a cube source and fails when it is `None`; its `Lognormal`-to-shifted promotion from source metadata and its `1e-12` displacement tolerance do not apply to cubes, so a caller pricing off a shifted cube must assert that shift. Of the request builders, the two swaption sites and inflation cap/floor always pass a convention (the swaption builds `Lognormal` or shifted from `vol_model` and its own `sabr_params` shift and relies on the promotion for a shifted cube); `cap_floor/pricing/pricer.rs` can pass `None`. `valuations/rates/src/cms_spread_option/pricer.rs` calls `get_convention` to require unshifted Black quotes and must still refuse a cube (CORE-MARKET-16 d7 keeps that refusal). This is 2d step 8 moved forward; the evaluator's signature, boundary policy, shift rules and NaN paths stay for 2d. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d4, d21, d22; MODELS-ANALYTIC-16 d18; CORE-MARKET-16 d3
- **Differs from the old plan:** The old plan added guards to `implied_vol_normal` and kept it, `BETA_SNAP_TOL` and `atm_vol_normal`. The record deletes all three and the guards it asked for already exist in effect; the real change is one entry point with an explicit convention and no snap.

- **Tests:** Add: T = 0, negative, NaN, infinite each a validation error for all three conventions; F = K < 0 with β > 0 and no shift a validation error; β = 0 Normal across zero succeeds; β = 5e-5 gives a different answer from β = 0 (pins snap removal; `models/tests/production_audit.rs` has a `NORMAL-SNAP` case that asserts a β = 0.00005 cube resolves to Normal and matches the β-dispatched quote, and must be rewritten); `Lognormal` with a stored shift and `ShiftedLognormal` with a non-matching or absent shift error; `Some(0.0)` shift accepted only with `ShiftedLognormal { shift: 0.0 }` or Normal; ρ = ±1 rejected at construction; negative shift accepted; `VolSource::get_convention` on a cube errors and `resolve_volatility` with a cube source and no requested convention errors. Record reference: β = 1, ν = 0, α = 2, T = 6 Normal returns a computation error (zero bracket).

#### #4 SABR wing formula is uncorrected Hagan (2002) while claiming Obloj (2008) (Blocker)

- **Defect (verified in source):** Confirmed. `PreparedLognormal::evaluate` and `implied_vol_normal_unchecked` in `expansion.rs` both use z = (ν/α)·(F K)^((1−β)/2)·ln(F/K) with truncated log-moneyness polynomials in the leading factor, and comments call this the Obloj correction. `chi` blends a series and a closed form between |z| = 1e-5 and 1e-3 and has ρ-endpoint special cases; `chi_partials` rejects |ρ| > 0.99.
- **Record remedy:**

  *Sub-slice 2a (formula, shared by scalar and prepared paths)*
  1. Geometry from original inputs: signed Δ = F − K; L = log(x/y) with x = F+s, y = K+s, magnitude evaluated as log1p(|Δ|/min(x,y)), sign from Δ; M = max(x,y); log g = log M − |L|/2. Do not form x·y, and do not let two separately rounded shifted levels collapse a non-zero Δ. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d7
  2. Exact signed CEV distance with d = 1−β and ℓ = |L|: D = M^d · L · e(d ℓ), e(u) = −expm1(−u)/u, e(0) = 1. β = 1 gives D = L; β = 0 gives D = Δ. Remove the moneyness polynomials. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d8, d9
  3. Leading factors: Black and shifted Black P_B = α M^(−d)/e(d ℓ); positive-domain Normal P_N = α M^β e(ℓ)/e(d ℓ); exact β = 0 Normal P_N = α with no translated-level work and z = ν Δ/α even when a shift is stored. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d10, d13
  4. One skew factor for every output: z = ν D/α, R = z/χ(z), evaluated by the stable identity s2 = (1−ρ)(1+ρ), S = hypot(z−ρ, √s2), B = 1−ρz, W = S+B (for B < 0 use W = s2 z²/(S−B)), u = z/√(2W), R = √(2W)/(2·asinhc(u)). Delete `chi`'s series/blend/endpoint branches, `z_over_chi`'s series region and `1e-14` χ cutoff, and `ratio_partials`' two cutoffs. R = 1 exactly when ν = 0 or Δ = 0. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d11, d12, d13
  5. Time factor on the positive domain: a = α/g^d; P = d² for Black and shifted Black, P = −β(2−β) for Normal; C = [P a² + 6ρβ a ν + (2−3ρ²)ν²]/24. Exact β = 0 Normal keeps only the ν² term. Quote = (P_B or P_N)·R·(1 + T·C). MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d14
  6. Form the bracket 24 + P(a√T)² + 6ρβ(a√T)(ν√T) + (2−3ρ²)(ν√T)² in scale with compensated arithmetic; a non-positive or unresolved bracket is a computation error. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d15
  7. Constant-volatility reductions hold exactly: β = 0, ν = 0, Normal returns α; β = 1, ν = 0, Black or shifted Black returns α. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d16
  8. Prepared path: replace `PreparedLognormal` and the `PreparedSabrStrike::{Normal, Lognormal}` split (chosen by β) with one prepared evaluation keyed by the requested convention that reuses step 1 geometry and the same formula for value and (α, ν, ρ) partials. Use χ_z = 1/S, χ_ρ = z²/(S W); at z = 0, R_z = −ρ/2 and R_ρ = 0. Delete `chi_partials`' |ρ| > 0.99 refusal and its blended-kernel derivative. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d19
  9. Bind the convention in the two in-module consumers now, in their final shape. `SabrSmile` construction becomes checked and takes a required `VolatilityConvention` fixed for the smile's lifetime; ATM, strike and bulk quotes, `strike_from_delta`, `call_price` and `invert_call_price` switch on it instead of `beta < BETA_SNAP_TOL`. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d20; MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 d1, d2
  10. `SabrCalibrator::calibrate` takes one named request: original forward, paired strikes and vols, T, fixed β, quote convention, optional model displacement, fit mode (Free or Pinned, replacing `with_atm_pinning`). Delete `SabrShift` (None/Fixed/Auto), `STANDARD_SHIFTS`, `standard_shift`, `resolve_shift` and `with_shift`. Convention and displacement follow 13 d4; fit on raw F and K, never on pre-shifted copies (today `calibrate` shifts the inputs and refits). Vega weights, the α seed and the pin read the request's convention instead of β. Callers: `calibration/src/targets/vol.rs` (equity) passes `Lognormal` with no displacement and loses `SabrShift::Auto`; `calibration/src/targets/swaption.rs` passes its qualified convention and actual displacement. MODELS-ANALYTIC-14 d1 (request only), d2, d3, d24 and d25 (first sentences); MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d20
  11. Rewrite the module header in `expansion.rs` and `sabr/mod.rs`: exact CEV leading distance with the Hagan midpoint time correction; an asymptotic quote with no arbitrage, wing or expiry guarantee. MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d1, d18

  *Sub-slice 2b (rest of calibration; in scope because record 14 re-signatures the constructor and outcome of the calibrator 2a just changed, and its prepared objective and Jacobian are rebuilt on 2a's partials)*
  12. Immutable `SabrCalibrator` built from required checked controls. Delete `new`, `Default`, `high_precision`, `with_tolerance`, `with_max_iterations` and the mutable getters' backing fields. MODELS-ANALYTIC-14 d1 (controls)
  13. Admission before solving: at least three aligned finite strikes, finite strictly positive vols, record 13 domain for every row; sort by original strike for the solver, reject duplicates including signed zero, report errors in caller order. MODELS-ANALYTIC-14 d4, d5
  14. Objective: r_i = √w_i (σ_i − q_i)/q_i with fixed market-vega weights; Normal uses d_i = (F−K_i)/(q_i √T), relative quotes use d_i = log(x/y_i)/(q_i √T) + q_i √T/2; v_i = max(1e−8, exp(−(|d_i|−D)(|d_i|+D)/2)) with D = min|d_i|, w_i = v_i/Σv. No large-penalty substitution for an invalid model value (already landed in `53b0385d69`: the `1e6` sentinels are gone and the residual callbacks propagate errors; keep it that way). Today's weights come from `normalized_quote_weights`, which picks the vega family from β. MODELS-ANALYTIC-14 d6, d7, d8
  15. Acceptance is a required absolute `max_abs_quote_error` in quote units applied to every final observation; remove the relative `tolerance` cap. MODELS-ANALYTIC-14 d9
  16. Solver: the checked LM kernel with required residual-norm and step-norm tolerances and a per-start attempt budget. 14 d10 delegates to CORE-NUMERICS-06, which landed in shape in `53b0385d69` (`core/src/math/solver_multi.rs`: `LevenbergMarquardtSolver::new(residual_tolerance, step_tolerance, max_iterations) -> Result`, fallible flat row-major residual and Jacobian callbacks, augmented column-pivoted QR, `LmTerminationReason` with `DampingLimit`, `LmStats` with `accepted_steps`); the calibrator already calls it. What remains is the caller side: pass the request's own tolerances and budget in place of `self.tolerance.min(1e-10)` and the literal `1e-12` step tolerance (no implicit tightening). Explicit ν and ρ intervals with logistic maps and no clamps (removes the hard-coded `(0.001, 2.0)` and `(−0.99, 0.99)` and the `clamp(1e-9, 1−1e-9)`); free α = α_seed·exp(p). MODELS-ANALYTIC-14 d10, d11
  17. Seed and starts: α seed from the quote nearest F (lower strike on a tie): q·x^(1−β) for Black, q/x^β for positive-backbone Normal, q for β = 0 Normal. Nine starts at the 1/4, 1/2, 3/4 points of the supplied ν and ρ intervals, ν-major. Replaces `deterministic_sabr_starts`' fixed grids and `initial_alpha_guess`. MODELS-ANALYTIC-14 d12, d13
  18. Fit mode is explicit Free or Pinned. Pinned requires the exact K = F observation, a closed positive α bracket, α tolerance, update budget and native ATM tolerance; delete `find_atm_vol`'s linear-in-variance interpolation and the Newton α solve in `solve_alpha_for_atm`, and add no free-fit fallback (source has none today: `fit_atm_pinned` never calls `fit_free`). Check monotonicity of the ATM polynomial on the bracket, solve with the supplied-bracket Brent kernel, reprice the returned α. 14 d17 delegates to CORE-NUMERICS-05, which landed in shape in `53b0385d69` (`core/src/math/solver.rs`: `BrentSolver::new(tolerance, max_iterations) -> Result` and `solve_in_bracket` over a fallible `FnMut(f64) -> Result<f64>`); use it as it stands. MODELS-ANALYTIC-14 d14, d15, d16, d17
  19. Jacobian from 2a's partials through the actual transforms; pinned uses dα/dp = −(∂_p σ_ATM)/(∂_α σ_ATM), replacing the pinned path's numerical-Jacobian `solve_least_squares` call. MODELS-ANALYTIC-14 d18
  20. Failures per start stay visible; reprice every candidate, apply the cap, rank passes by recomputed norm, earlier start wins ties; preflight and count work. Delete `sabr_termination_is_acceptable`: since `53b0385d69` it rejects `MaxIterations` and `DampingLimit` outcomes, while the record lets a finite outcome with step, damping or attempt exhaustion pass the financial checks and keeps its literal solver status. MODELS-ANALYTIC-14 d19, d20, d21
  21. `SabrCalibrationOutcome` reshaped: winning start index and physical start, solver termination and step counts, signed errors in input order, max absolute error, weighted norm, call counts, rejected causes, plus convention, displacement, β, forward, expiry, controls and mode. Remove `max_relative_quote_error` and `parameters_at_bounds`. Validate on restore. MODELS-ANALYTIC-14 d22, d23
  22. Callers pass the explicit controls: the equity target its native quote cap, the swaption target its chosen mode and controls; neither forces a pin from a quote tag (today the swaption target forces β = 0 and `with_atm_pinning(true)` for a Normal tag and rejects a non-positive shifted-lognormal shift, both of which 14 d25 removes). MODELS-ANALYTIC-14 d24, d25 (first sentences only; see Deferred)
  23. Host exception documentation (handed over from Slice 7, audit #33). #4 step 10 already rewrites the `calibrate` stub and JSDoc for the request object in 2a, and the corrected exception classes go in with that rewrite; this step extends them to 2b's failure causes. A request fault is `Error::Validation`; "no start passes" is `Error::Calibration`, whose `kind()` is `ErrorKind::Computation`. `finstack-quant-py/src/errors.rs` maps Validation to `ValueError` and Computation to `RuntimeError` (`core_to_py`, which the binding uses); the WASM error kind is `computation`. #### #16 (SabrSmile part) missing vertical and intrinsic bounds in smile validation and repair (Major)

- **Defect (verified in source):** Confirmed in `finstack-quant/models/src/volatility/sabr/smile.rs`. `post_repair_validation` checks only C_i ≤ C_(i−1) and convexity (with a factor of two and a hidden `1e-6` tolerance). There is no upper vertical bound, no intrinsic bound, no lower-support anchor. `repair_arbitrage` is an iterated greedy clamp with a fixed `1e-8` price step and a `max_iterations` argument. Pricing discounts with a caller `r`. The quote family is picked by `beta < BETA_SNAP_TOL`. `SabrSmile::new` is unchecked; `log_moneyness` returns NaN outside its domain.
- **Record remedy (sub-slice 2c):**
  1. (Done in 2a: checked construction with a required convention, 15 d1–2.) Here: bulk quotes are atomic and order-preserving; the smile stays runtime-only. MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 d1, d2
  2. Replace `log_moneyness` with a checked convention-specific axis: K − F for Normal, log((K+s)/(F+s)) for Black and shifted Black; no NaN. Python `to_dataframe` shows `strike_offset` or `log_moneyness` accordingly. MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 d3
  3. Rename `strike_from_delta` to `strike_from_atm_delta` with explicit call/put, delta in (0,1) and `delta_rel_tol` in (0,1). Normal: K = F − η w z; Black: e = w(w/2 − η z), K = x·exp(e) − s, restored as F + x·expm1(e) near equal levels. Validate the returned K against the record 13 domain, then recheck the smaller delta tail in log space within log1p(±delta_rel_tol). No clipping. MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 d4, d5, d6, d7
  4. Grid admission: at least two finite strictly ascending strikes in the quote domain, finite non-negative `price_abs_tol`; validate before pricing; no sorting. MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 d8
  5. Price undiscounted unit calls from the checked quotes with the forward-price kernel; delete the `r` argument and the `df` in `call_price`/`invert_call_price`. The price function is record 02's (`forward-normal-shifted.md`), which Slice 3 #14 implements and which precedes this slice in the proposed order: 2c calls the unit-scale `black76_price` (its `df` argument removed there), `bachelier_price` and `black_shifted_price` (signed shift) in `models/src/closed_form/forward.rs` as #14 leaves them, in place of today's direct `black_call`/`bachelier_call` on pre-shifted levels. If 2c started before #14, it would be written against the `df`-taking kernel and touched again. MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 d9
  6. Anchor: Black prepends (0, F); shifted Black prepends (−s, F+s); Normal has none, including Normal on a displaced backbone. Never request a SABR quote at the anchor. MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 d10
  7. Bounds: C_i ≥ max(F − K_i, 0) for all; Black and shifted Black also C_i ≤ F + s. MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 d11
  8. Vertical and convexity: 0 ≤ C_i − C_(i+1) ≤ K_(i+1) − K_i for each adjacent pair; C_i ≤ (g_R C_(i−1) + g_L C_(i+1))/(g_L + g_R) for each triple; include the anchor in both; excess reported in unit premium; delete the factor two and `severity_pct`. MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 d12
  9. One tolerance: a violation is an exact positive excess greater than `price_abs_tol`; report kind, coordinates, excess, whether the anchor was used and the pair/triple counts. Comparisons use exact arithmetic on the represented binary64 values via the existing `num-bigint` dependency (present in `finstack-quant/core/Cargo.toml`; add to `models` or place the helper in core). MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 d13, d14
  10. Repair is explicit and down-only: target is the greatest grid at or below the raw calls meeting bounds, vertical and convexity rules with the anchor fixed; a raw call below intrinsic fails. Compute L_i = min_{j≤i} C_j, R_i = min(C_i, R_(i+1) + K_(i+1) − K_i), M_i = min(L_i, R_i), then the lower convex hull of (K_i, M_i) with exact slope comparison and chord fill. Delete the `max_iterations` argument and the `1e-8` step. MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 d15, d16, d17, d18
  11. Required `max_abs_reduction` in unit premium; reject before any inversion if any C_i − C_i* exceeds it. MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 d19
  12. Restore vols only at changed nodes with the accepted inverse and explicit IV controls; target at intrinsic gives zero vol; an interior target rounding onto a bound errors. Reprice all returned vols, require each at or below its raw call, inside the move cap and within `price_abs_tol` of the hull target, and rerun the grid checker; any failure returns an error with no partial result. MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 d20 (delegates the inverse and its controls to record 03, `implied-volatility-solvers.md`, which no slice in this plan implements; 2c would call today's `implied_vol_black` and `implied_vol_bachelier`, see open question 3), d21
  13. Output: one grid report type and one repair artifact (strikes, convention, displacement, repaired vols, change flags, target and repriced premiums, reductions, post-repair report), output-only (no `Deserialize`), verdict derived from findings. Delete `ArbitrageValidationResult`, `ButterflyViolation`, `MonotonicityViolation`, `validate_no_arbitrage`, `check_no_arbitrage`, `worst_butterfly_severity`. MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 d22, d24
  14. Error kinds: request faults are validation errors; infeasible target, failed inverse or postcheck keep their computation/convergence cause. MODELS-ANALYTIC-15 d23
- **Differs from the old plan:** The anchor, vertical bound, intrinsic bound and unequal-gap convexity match (15 d10–12). The old plan bolted them onto the existing greedy `repair` and kept `r`, `max_iterations`, the hidden tolerance and the β-derived quote family; the record replaces the repair with a slope-limited pass plus convex hull, makes tolerance and move cap required inputs, removes discounting and replaces the result types.
- **Files:** Rust `models/src/volatility/sabr/{smile.rs, tests.rs, mod.rs}`, `models/src/schema.rs` (drop `ArbitrageValidationResult`, register the new report types), `models/Cargo.toml` if `num-bigint` is added. Python `src/bindings/models/volatility.rs` (`PySabrSmile`: constructor, `strike_from_atm_delta`, grid check, repair, `to_dataframe`), `finstack_quant/models/volatility/__init__.pyi`. WASM `src/api/models/volatility.rs` (`JsSabrSmile`), `index.d.ts` via `gen-write`. Parity: `SabrSmile` members and the `types` map in `parity_contract.toml`. Schema: delete `models/schemas/models/1/arbitrage_validation_result.schema.json`, add the new ones, regenerate `types/generated/models/models.ts`. UI: `finstack-quant-ui/registry/shared/file/services/models.ts` (builds `SabrSmile` from cube-node parameters), `registry/models/hooks/use-cube-smile/use-cube-smile.ts`, `registry/models/components/vol-cube-explorer/vol-cube-explorer.tsx`, `tests/models/cube-samples.test.ts`. Host tests: `tests/models/{test_audit_regressions.py, test_closed_form_rust_owned.py}`, notebook `sabr_volatility.ipynb`. Unrelated same-name hits to leave alone: `calibration/src/validation/curves.rs` `validate_no_arbitrage` is a curve trait.

Add: Black grid whose first observed call exceeds F is flagged through the anchor; vertical excess with C_i − C_(i+1) > K_(i+1) − K_i flagged; call below intrinsic flagged by the checker and failing repair; unequal-gap convexity; two-strike Normal grid has zero triples; hull result equals an independent constrained solution on an irregular grid; `max_abs_reduction = 0` passes a clean grid and rejects a dirty one before inversion; repaired vols reprice at or below raw calls. Delta proxy, from the record: F = −0.002, T = 1, ATM Normal vol 0.006 gives 25-delta call and put strikes ≈ 0.002046938501 and −0.006046938501; shifted Black, s = 0.03, ATM vol 0.2 gives ≈ 0.002691086617 and −0.005039200852, 50-delta ≈ −0.001434362479; Normal output on β = 0.5, α = 0.1, ν = ρ = 0, F = 0.001, T = 1 has ATM ≈ 0.002174065891 and a 25-delta put proxy outside the positive backbone, which must error.

#### #16 (VolCube part) heterogeneous beta/shift blending in the cube (Major)

- **Defect (verified in source):** Partly fixed since the audit. Already landed in `cda46a9a6d` in `finstack-quant/models/src/volatility/source.rs`, `cube_params`: nearest-node β selection and its clamp, the `1e-8` α floor, the ±0.9999 ρ cap and the index-0 fallback after a failed `segment` are gone; the active corners must share one β by exact comparison or the call errors, and `segment` collapses an exact coordinate to one index, so an exact node now reads its own tuple. Still there: α, ν, ρ and forward are bilinearly blended into a struct literal that bypasses validation; an absent shift becomes 0 when any active neighbor has one; `cube_params` clamps its coordinates unconditionally; `get_cube_vol_clamped` returns NaN on failure; `VolSource::get_convention` infers a cube's quote units from β (removed in 2a); `normal_output` rejects a shifted-lognormal request; `cube_total_variance` rejects `total <= 0`. New since `cda46a9a6d` and against the record: `cube_total_variance` calls `cube_params` across both expiry rows first, so TotalVariance mode now refuses rows with different β, which 16 d17 allows. The materializers evaluate through the NaN-returning clamped helper, fill absent shifts with `unwrap_or(0.0)`, copy the cube's mode onto the output grid, and the expiry-slice one clamps the requested expiry.
- **Record remedy (sub-slice 2d):**
  1. One checked cube evaluator taking expiry, tenor, absolute strike, a required output quote family and an explicit boundary policy (strict or flat clamp of the stored axes only; the original strike is kept; clamp uses the effective expiry in the SABR kernel). The record selects the family with the existing `VolQuoteType` (Normal, Black or shifted Black), not with a `VolatilityConvention` as `get_cube_vol` takes today; the evaluator forms the `VolatilityConvention` from the actual shift, returns it with sigma and the requested and evaluated coordinates, and checks any caller convention or displacement assertion against it exactly. Replaces `get_cube_vol` and `get_cube_vol_clamped`; no NaN path. MODELS-ANALYTIC-16 d3, d6, d16; CORE-MARKET-16 d3, d4
  2. Support before weights: an exact coordinate selects one stored index, an interior coordinate its adjacent pair; evaluate only that Cartesian product and check every active corner. `segment` already collapses exact coordinates; what is missing is weights formed from the original coordinates in checked scale and no clamp before support selection. MODELS-ANALYTIC-16 d7, d8
  3. Exact node: use the stored α, β, ν, ρ, shift Option and construction forward directly, with no neighbor contamination, and evaluate record 13 at the original strike. The floor and cap are already gone (`cda46a9a6d`); read the node rather than a four-way blend of itself, and pin it with a test. MODELS-ANALYTIC-16 d14; CORE-MARKET-16 d1, d5
  4. ParameterSpace mode: blend α, ν, ρ and construction forward with the expiry-by-tenor weights; require one β across the active nodes by exact equality (no tolerance) and keep it (already enforced since `cda46a9a6d`, which also deleted nearest-β selection, the α floor and the ρ cap; keep the check and its `mixed_beta_nodes_preserve_endpoints_and_reject_midpoint_queries` test). The blended tuple is validated through the checked `SabrParameters` constructor (done in #3 step 10). MODELS-ANALYTIC-16 d15; CORE-MARKET-16 d5
  5. Shift in ParameterSpace mode: all absent gives absent; all present blends as a signed parameter with the same weights and stays present even at zero; mixed presence is an error. MODELS-ANALYTIC-16 d16
  6. TotalVariance mode: on each active expiry row apply the tenor rule of steps 4–5, evaluate record 13 at that row's own expiry and forward at the same absolute strike, then combine rows as sqrt(Σ w_i T_i σ_i² / T_effective); exact expiry returns the row. Rows may differ in β and forward, so delete the cross-row `cube_params` preflight that `cda46a9a6d` added to `cube_total_variance`. Replace its `total <= 0` rejection with: a mathematically zero blend returns zero, a lost positive or non-finite result errors. MODELS-ANALYTIC-16 d17, d10, d11
  7. Output family across rows: Normal allows rows with different shift Options and carries no displacement; Black requires absent shift on every row; shifted Black requires the same signed displacement on every evaluated row. The single-tuple form of this rule, and the removal of `normal_output`'s refusal of a shifted request, land in #3 step 10; here it is applied per evaluated row. MODELS-ANALYTIC-16 d18; CORE-MARKET-16 d3
  8. (Done in #3 step 10: `VolSource::get_convention` for a cube is an error and `resolve_volatility` passes its `VolatilityRequest.convention` for cube sources, failing when it is `None`.) Here: the cube arms of `get_vol`, `get_vol_clamped`, `get_normal_vol`, `get_normal_vol_clamped` route through step 1 with the caller's selection and boundary policy, and `resolve_volatility` takes the convention the evaluator returns instead of assembling it. MODELS-ANALYTIC-16 d3; CORE-MARKET-16 d3; MODELS-ANALYTIC-13 d21
  9. Name the cube modes ParameterSpace and TotalVariance and make the choice mandatory at construction, replacing the shared `VolInterpolationMode::Vol` label on `VolCube` (core `market_data/surfaces/vol_cube.rs`, `with_interpolation_mode`). Surfaces keep `VolInterpolationMode`. CORE-MARKET-16 d2; MODELS-ANALYTIC-16 d15, d17
  10. Materializers `materialize_cube_tenor_slice` and `materialize_cube_expiry_slice`: require output ID, quote family (`VolQuoteType`, replacing the `VolatilityConvention` argument), output-grid interpolation (chosen by the caller, not copied from the cube's mode as today), the fixed tenor or expiry inside the source domain (the expiry-slice materializer clamps it today), and a non-empty ordered unique strike grid; evaluate every node through step 1 under strict policy; fail atomically; carry the actual per-row displacement (no `unwrap_or(0.0)`), consistent within each row. MODELS-ANALYTIC-16 d20, d21 (shape and displacement only); CORE-MARKET-16 d9
- **Differs from the old plan:** The old plan rejected corners whose β or shift differed beyond `1e-12` and kept everything else. The record requires exact β equality, blends shifts when all are present and fails only on mixed presence, lets TotalVariance mode combine rows with different β, removes the clamp and NaN paths, and requires the output selection on every cube evaluation. Source moved toward the record in `cda46a9a6d` (exact β, no floor or cap), so the remaining work is the shift rule, the TotalVariance preflight, the boundary policy, the mode names and the materializers.

The rest (2b) is roughly a rewrite of `calibration.rs` (1,708 lines) and could be its own slice after 2a with no stopgap left behind, at the cost of re-signaturing the calibrator's constructor and outcome a second time. It is in 2d because the evaluator dispatches on it. Acceptable, or leave the stored tag as is and defer the rename with CORE-MARKET-16 d6/d11?

After it, a cap/floor request that passes no convention against a cube fails, and a Black swaption priced off a shifted cube fails unless it asserts the cube's shift (today the resolver promotes it silently). Accept both changes in 2a, and confirm that the caplet path (tenor 0 with clamp, CORE-MARKET-16 d7, deferred) may keep selecting a cube's first tenor until its own record lands.
9. **Cube output selector.** 16 d3 selects the cube output with `VolQuoteType` and returns the resolved `VolatilityConvention`; 13 d4 requires `VolatilityConvention` on the scalar quote. 2d follows both as written, which changes the argument type of `get_cube_vol` and the materializers. Confirm.

#### #7 `ToggleExerciseModel::OptimalExercise` nested equity comparison (Blocker)

- **Defect (verified in source):** `finstack-quant/models/src/credit/toggle_exercise.rs`: `optimal_toggle_decision` uses one `coupon_due` for both the cash outflow and the PIK addition, clamps a negative coupon, infers missing assets from leverage or `2 * notional`, returns PIK on a cash-leg origin breach before testing the PIK leg, derives the nested seed from `u.to_bits()` (`uniform_to_seed`), hardcodes `NESTED_STEPS_PER_YEAR = 12`, uses one PCG stream per path, and compares two separately accumulated payoff sums. `CreditState` derives `Default`, has `#[serde(default)] coupon_due` and no `deny_unknown_fields`; `extract_state_value` reads a missing distance as `0.0`. Caller `finstack-quant/valuations/fixed-income/src/bond/pricing/merton_mc/engine.rs` falls back to cash when no toggle model is set, to `0.5` for a missing uniform, to `dd = 0.0`, and to a Merton-PD-equivalent hazard when no leverage map is configured. Host side: Python `CreditState(...)` defaults every field (`0.0` or `None`), while WASM `shouldPik` reads the state as raw serde with only `coupon_due` defaulted. Slice 0 step 0c-3 changes two details before this starts (`Pcg64Rng::new_with_stream` becomes `Pcg64Rng::new(seed, stream)`; `should_pik` returns `Result<bool>`); everything else above stands.
- **Record vs audit (read this first):** the audit's core complaint is the flat PIK barrier `N + C` with no PIK accrual. The record keeps that: each action's debt is "that action's flat modeled barrier and terminal strike" (CREDIT-05 d7), and when both legs survive the paired difference is `C*exp(Y) − P` (CREDIT-05 d9). With `C = P`, `r > 0` and negligible default risk the mean is `C*(exp(rT) − 1) > 0`, so the record's model still elects PIK for a healthy firm. The record's answer is that `C` and `P` are separate product inputs and that the rule is a hypothetical single-election comparison with no claim about issuer behavior. The old plan's `pik_rate` / `pik_coupon_due` compounding is dropped: no accepted decision supplies it.
- **Record remedy:**
  1. Rename the variant `OptimalExercise` to `EquityComparison` (serde tag `equity_comparison`), with its config struct and constructor renamed to match; delete `optimal`. CREDIT-05 d7.
  2. Replace `CreditState` (mutable, `Default`, optional fields) with checked immutable per-rule input: a feature rule receives only its selected feature (annual hazard finite `>= 0`, debt-to-assets leverage finite `>= 0`, or a finite signed distance with a caller-identified basis); the equity comparison receives `V > 0`, `N > 0`, `C >= 0`, `P >= 0` on one monetary basis. A missing required feature is an error. Delete the `Default` derive, the `coupon_due` serde default, missing-distance-as-zero, asset inference, and the negative-coupon clamp. Deleting `CreditState` is the resolution of the Python/WASM constructor and field mismatch handed over from Slice 7: remove the Python class with its export and stub, the WASM `generated.models.CreditState` parameter type, `credit_state.schema.json` with its registry entry, and both parity entries. Neither host keeps a constructor to align. CREDIT-05 d5, d7.
  3. Threshold: finite threshold, strict `>` / `<`, equality chooses cash, no uniform argument. Logistic: finite intercept and sensitivity, checked fused predictor `η = intercept + sensitivity*x`, explicit finite `u` in `[0,1)`; elect PIK when `log(u) − log1p(−u) < η` for `u > 0`, and always at `u = 0`; no materialized sigmoid. In scope because steps 2 and 6 re-signature the one decision entry point all three rules share. CREDIT-05 d6.
  4. Equity-comparison config requires finite positive horizon, positive step count, positive path count, finite continuous `r`, finite `σ >= 0`, explicit `u64` seed and stream, and a positive work budget; check `paths*steps` against the budget before execution. Delete `NESTED_STEPS_PER_YEAR` and `uniform_to_seed`. CREDIT-05 d8.
  5. Simulation: canonical uniform grid with exact horizon and actual adjacent widths, built as `TimeGrid::uniform(horizon, steps)` (Slice 0 step 0a) and read through `get_dts()`, replacing `horizon / n_steps`; zero-payout GBM log increment `(r − σ²/2)Δt + σ sqrt(Δt) Z`; one `Pcg64Rng::new(seed, stream)` per decision with normals from the fallible `normal`; one common normal per path-step in path-major order regardless of which leg is alive; touch or breach at origin or any knot (maturity included) extinguishes that leg; no between-knot correction. `σ = 0` uses one deterministic pair and no draws. Both legs breached at origin, or `C = P = 0`, is a known tie with no draws. CREDIT-05 d8; CN-20 d4-5 (the grid profile d8 links); CN-17 d2, d5 (the PCG stream d8 names).
  6. Origin tests are inclusive and independent: cash leg breached when `V − C <= N`, PIK leg when `V <= N + P`; a breached leg has zero equity; the two tests stay mathematically equal when `C = P`. CREDIT-05 d7.
  7. Decision: both legs alive contributes `C*exp(Y) − P`, computed as `(C − P) + C*expm1(Y)` near zero; one leg alive contributes that leg's `max(V_T − B, 0)` with sign; stream the checked compensated paired mean; no discount factor. Elect PIK only when the mean is strictly positive; exact tie (two zero legs included) chooses cash. CREDIT-05 d9.
  8. The decision entry point (already `Result<bool>` after Slice 0 step 0c-3) returns the action, the mean undiscounted difference, both origin-breach flags and the work/replay controls for the equity comparison; unresolved arithmetic or work exhaustion fails the whole decision. CREDIT-05 d9.
  9. Wire: strict tagged state with the selected variant's semantic fields only; reject unknown fields, invalid levels, missing state and mismatched controls; exact `u64` seed and stream cross Python, WASM and `instrument_pricing_overrides`. CREDIT-05 d10.
  10. Bond caller: a `PikMode::Toggle` coupon without a toggle model is an error (delete the cash fallback); a missing logistic uniform is an error (delete `unwrap_or(0.5)`); delete the PD-equivalent hazard fallback and the `dd = 0.0` fallback. Admitted features are accreted debt/asset leverage, the configured leverage-intensity map, or a positive-horizon terminal-GBM distance; any other combination fails. Propagate toggle and hazard errors out of the path closure with no partial result. CREDIT-05 d10, BONDS-08 d6.
  11. Bond caller supplies `C` and `P` explicitly (today both are `coupon_amount`; "product input may select equal amounts explicitly") and an explicit per-election seed/stream for the nested comparison, independent of outer draw consumption. CREDIT-05 d7, BONDS-08 d6, BONDS-08 d7 (nested-comparison sentence only).
- **Differs from the old plan:** old plan kept the variant name, added `pik_rate` compounding, tie-broke only the both-breached origin case and seeded PCG from a state hash. Record renames the variant, keeps flat barriers with separate `C` and `P`, requires explicit counts/seed/stream/budget, decides on the paired difference, and makes the call fallible.
