#### Open questions for the owner

1. **Fallible step.** In-scope records also demand per-step error propagation (MC-04 d2, MC-06 d7, MC-15 d5/d7), but `Discretization::step` is infallible for every scheme. Slice 0 step 0c-2 makes the draws fallible, not `step`. This slice adds only the pre-draw admission hook. Confirm a fallible `step` belongs to the engine (MC-01) slice.
2. **Sobol result shape.** MC-03 d7 makes SE/CI unavailable but is silent on the type. Optional `stderr`/`ci_95`/`relative_stderr` on `Estimate`/`MoneyEstimate` touches 135 `.stderr` lines in 48 Rust files (120 lines in 45 workspace files, 15 in the 3 Python/WASM binding files) plus the `.pyi`, facade and four schemas; a separate Sobol result avoids that but gives `PathDependentPricer::price` two return shapes. Which?
3. **One strict solver, many owners.** MC-24 d6 = MA-26 d17 is a single solver with explicit `tau` and iteration limit. #10 and #11 cannot land without also changing the HW swaption LSMC, LMM Bermudan and bond polynomial callers (records valuations-rates 08/09 and valuations-bonds 07, not read for this slice): each must supply `tau` and loses truncation, and the bond degree ladder depends on it. Land those owners in the same slice, or sequence this slice after them?
4. **`Hw1fMcConfig` split.** MA-25 d17 and MA-26 d19 strip `oos_lsmc`, `basis_degree` and parity `SampleSplit` from the HW1F driver, but the holder swaption and LMM routes still use them. Proposal: plain-driver and issuer control structs in `models`; the parity fields move verbatim to a rates-owned struct until records 08/09 are implemented. And where do the callable range accrual's explicit training/pricing counts come from: pricer config or instrument override?
5. **`r0`.** MA-25 d1 removes the initial-rate input, which needs the deferred centered-state rewrite. Confirm the non-finite `r0` gap stays open until then rather than adding a check that is deleted later.
6. **Exact knots vs the grid builder.** Answered by Slice 0 0a: `uniform_with_required_times` becomes an exact union (CN-20 d6), so a required break or jump is always a stored knot and `get_index` finds it; this slice lands after 0a and builds no grid of its own. What remains for the owner: (i) a break or jump one ulp from a base knot becomes a real ulp-wide step with its own draw (Slice 0 0a open question 2), which MC-06 d5 and d7 accept ("however close", "widths below the old cutoff") and MC-04 d5 does not forbid (it removes the absolute spacing floor); confirm no owner minimum applies on the piecewise and dividend routes; (ii) removing the callers' `1e-10` dedup means the same date listed twice is one checkpoint, two schedule dates that map to nearly equal times are both kept and both bootstrapped, and distinct dates that map to the same time error (MC-04 d5): confirm that is the wanted behaviour for autocallable and cliquet schedules.
7. **GBM scheme retirement here?** Steps 16-17 remove the `milstein` wire tag and `gbm`/`multi_gbm` Euler selections (schema, `.pyi`, test and notebook fallout). Forced by MC-04 d1, but could ride with a dedicated MC-05 slice if sub-part (d) is deferred whole.
8. **Sobol adapter timing.** Slice 0 0c-2 adapts `impl RandomStream for SobolRng` to the fallible signatures and #18 step 5 deletes it. Step 5 (adapter module, `is_quasi_random`, the engine rejection, two engine tests) depends on nothing else in this slice. Proposal: move step 5 into 0c-2 so nothing is adapted and then deleted; otherwise it lands first in this slice.
9. **`LsmcPricer` constructor shape.** Adding `tau` and the iteration limit takes `LsmcPricer::new` from 7 to 9 inputs, which the `too_many_arguments` rule turns into one params struct (Rust) and one params object (Python, WASM). That is a host-visible re-signature MC-24 d10 does not spell out, and MC-23 d13 and MC-24 d2 re-shape the same constructor again later (single two-pass `price_american`, basis family removed). Land the params object now with today's fields plus the two fit controls, or pull MC-24 d2/d10 and MC-23 d13 into this slice so the host constructor changes once?
10. **Bridge numerics have no owner.** CN-17 d7 is step 9a. CN-17 d8, d10, d11 and MC-03 d5 are deferred as one unit; after MC-03 d5 the model route computes standardized increments privately and may stop calling `construct_path`, leaving core's level arithmetic without a production caller. Take the unit into this slice (bridge-route Sobol prices then re-pin once, with the block layout), or name the slice that owns it?
11. **Slice 0 hand-off mismatch.** Slice 0 0a's Deferred list assigns the `dt(0)` caches of `qe_heston`, `exact_hw1f` and `schwartz_smith` to #18 step 12. Step 12 covers only the piecewise, dividend and rough-Heston schemes; the three caches are bit-match guarded and stay correct, and their records are outside this slice. Leave them to those records (the fast path can miss on uniform grids after 0a until then), or add them here after reading those records?

## Final gate

After every slice has passed its own `mise run slice-gate`:

1. `mise run gen-write`
2. `mise run all-fmt`
3. `mise run all`
